Research & Engineering Lineage

Lean

Lean: outcomes, flow and the conditions for useful improvement

Lean examines how work creates an outcome for someone, how that work moves through a system, and how people can detect and improve problems. The current study treats production flow, learning, participation, resilience and economic choice as distinct functions. It does not make a board, workshop, stock target or short cycle sufficient evidence of improvement. Its forty current property records preserve the forty historical identities while qualifying what each claim actually establishes.

What the current study finds

An outcome needs a named beneficiary, a time horizon and constraints that cannot be exchanged for a faster local metric. Flow must include the actual demand-to-outcome path: moving a queue outside the visible work area can shorten the displayed cycle time while leaving total waiting unchanged. A work-in-progress limit matters when it genuinely controls admission to a defined population. It does not create capacity or prove that a customer receives value.

Buffers have equally concrete meanings. Suitable inventory, time or capacity can absorb specified variability and consequences. Better information can improve the choice of reserve, but cannot become physical stock or a feasible recovery path. Full utilization is not inherently harmful: a deterministic, appropriately matched process can have no queue at full utilization. Variability and service conditions determine whether the familiar waiting problem arises. These distinctions support choosing an adequate control, including a larger economic batch or an unchanged arrangement.

Two examples that preserve the limits

A visible queue can fall when admission is restricted even as work waits longer before admission. The relevant comparison includes both queues and the same completion objective; a prettier board alone does not show improvement.

A rollout confined to fewer genuinely independent instances can reduce the directly exposed population under the same local fault, even if recovery fails. That is a useful partial benefit. It does not establish acceptable loss, exposure duration, total risk or authority to deploy. The accepted study separates this exposure argument from its additional qualification and recovery cases.

Learning and participation require real functions

A detector, a transmitted signal, an acknowledged concern, containment, restoration and prevention are different accomplishments. Nominal human presence does not demonstrate effective exception handling. Likewise, a reporting channel does not establish a worker's right to be heard, protection against retaliation or the ability to obtain a response. A competent inquiry may support keeping a useful baseline unchanged; improvement is not measured by how many changes or workshops occur.

Evidence, genealogy and open questions

The genealogy distinguishes documented Toyota, TWI, production, supplier and later software or service relationships from analogy and uncertain transmission. Labour and ergonomic adversity remains part of the record. The source study separates doctrine, testimony, observation and analytical models rather than treating every citation as an independent causal test.

The eight negative or ceremonial forms reject universal zero stock, maximum utilization, smallest batch and tool conformity, while retaining valid scoped uses. Two examined novelty candidates were already expressible through existing properties; no new irreducible property was established. Buffer shorthand and one duplicate ceremony-row mismatch remain explicit qualification advisories. The finite cases do not measure universal productivity, worker wellbeing or operational benefit.

EL-001
id

EL-001

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-001

PROPERTY_NAME

Outcome and value definition with named beneficiary, constraints and time horizon

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota's customer-first quality doctrine; Lean Thinking's value principle; later service/product critiques.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

LEAN_PRODUCTION_GENERALISATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Define value from the customer and orient production toward it.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Organisations can efficiently produce outputs that do not improve the beneficiary's outcome or that externalise cost.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Efficient delivery of low-value, harmful or locally beneficial work.

MECHANISM

Name the beneficiary and desired outcome; state safety, labour, resilience and externality constraints; choose a time horizon; test whether work changes the outcome.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Multiple plausible outputs compete, resources are material, or local efficiency could displace customer/system value.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For trivial, reversible work, record a one-sentence purpose and acceptance condition rather than create a value-stream artefact.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Legitimate beneficiary representation; outcome observability; explicit non-negotiable constraints; authority to stop low-value work.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Better selection, fewer abandoned outputs, reduced proxy optimisation and clearer trade-offs.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Customer rhetoric masks managerial cost goals; one stakeholder's value harms another; short-horizon metrics; unmeasurable outcomes; retrospective rationalisation.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Lean literature specifies waste more richly than value; customer language can ignore workers, suppliers, public risk and long-term resilience.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Moved from production value and waste categories toward multi-stakeholder outcome framing, product discovery and system constraints.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Use an explicit value hypothesis with beneficiary, outcome, constraints, evidence threshold, review date and abandonment rule.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

No Lean vocabulary, value workshop or value-stream map is required; the property is a decision-relevant outcome model.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should value be aggregated when customers, workers, suppliers and public safety conflict?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

SYSTEM_OWNER

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-009

  2. EL-023

  3. EL-008

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the target system name the beneficiary, desired outcome, constraints and time horizon rather than equating output or waste removal with value?

  2. Can it stop or retire work when the value hypothesis fails?

  3. Are labour, safety, resilience and external costs prevented from disappearing behind a customer-value claim?

bearer

scientific_origin

Normative/analytical outcome framing informed by Lean value doctrine

current_evidence_warrant

S010/S011 and R008 establish doctrine; heterogeneous domain findings do not prove that a value-statement format causes benefit. S060 is narrowed by R005.

incremental_control_cost

Time to identify affected parties and observe outcomes; expensive measurement can exceed trivial work's value.

qualitative_evaluation_criterion

State whose outcome is sought and which constraints cannot be traded away; leave effects unknown when only throughput is measured.

EL-002
id

EL-002

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-002

PROPERTY_NAME

End-to-end system boundary and flow/queue/handoff visibility

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

TPS material/information flow; Lean value-stream generalisation; later service/software translations.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

LEAN_PRODUCTION_GENERALISATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

See the whole path from demand through production and delivery rather than optimise isolated machines or departments.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Local gains create downstream queues, rework, handoff loss or delayed customer outcomes.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Local optimisation and invisible waiting across organisational boundaries.

MECHANISM

Define the relevant path and work classes; observe arrivals, queues, processing, rework, handoffs, dependencies and outcome latency; assign end-to-end ownership.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Repeated handoffs, unexplained elapsed time, departmental optimisation or customer delay dominate processing time.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For a short single-owner path, a simple trace or sampled timeline is enough; no wall-sized map.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Stable item identity; traceability across boundaries; truthful timestamps/states; an owner able to resolve cross-boundary causes.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Reduced total lead time, fewer handoff failures, better bottleneck and queue decisions.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Map theatre; stale diagrams; excessive instrumentation; forced linear path for exploratory work; omission of externalities; local teams unable to change system constraints.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Value-stream maps are frequently local snapshots and can mistake visible factory-like sequence for the real distributed causal system.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Value-stream mapping was generalised beyond factories; modern software combines traces and delivery telemetry, while discovery work may require networks rather than linear streams.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Maintain the cheapest decision-useful end-to-end model, including feedback/rework loops, queues, dependencies and outcome—not just touch time.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A value-stream map is one implementation. Event logs, service blueprints, dependency graphs or sampled traces may be stronger.

CURRENT_STATUS

STRONGLY_RETAINED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_CONVERGENT

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S003

  2. S005

  3. S018

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S060

  2. S065

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S020

  2. S061

  3. S058

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should boundaries be chosen in multi-product, platform or ecosystem settings without creating an unmanageable total map?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

CROSS_FUNCTIONAL

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

End-to-end latency, queue and handoff profile; assumes valid item identity and boundary. Over-instrumentation and forced linearisation are failure modes.

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-018

  2. EL-029

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the system expose end-to-end waiting, queues, handoffs, rework and outcome latency rather than only local activity?

  2. Is the map or trace current enough to change a decision?

  3. Does it preserve a cheap path where the work is already short, observable and single-owner?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

EL-003

EL-003

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-003

PROPERTY_NAME

Workplace and work-state legibility

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

TPS visual control/5S/andon; later service and software visualisation.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Arrange physical work so expected state, ownership, missing items and abnormalities are easy to discern.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Search, ambiguity and hidden state delay action or conceal abnormal work.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Undetected abnormality, lost work, excessive search and ambiguous ownership.

MECHANISM

Expose state and limits at the point of work using labels, layout, telemetry, state models or visual cues proportional to decision need.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Work is repeatedly lost, searched for, misrouted, duplicated or left in an ambiguous state.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

When state is already obvious and low-risk, use naming conventions or a small status field; do not create ritual boards or audit scoring.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Shared state definitions; maintenance ownership; accessibility; protection against surveillance and stale displays.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Faster recognition, lower search/coordination cost, earlier abnormality detection.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

5S audit theatre; cosmetic tidiness; information overload; stale boards; worker surveillance; local legibility hiding end-to-end state.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Evidence for exact 5S bundles is small and heterogeneous; legibility can become discipline and scoring rather than aid.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Expanded from physical workplace order to digital work-state and observability; mature form separates human legibility from metric control.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Make the minimum relevant normal/abnormal state visible to people who can act, with freshness, accessibility and ownership controls.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

5S labels, shadow boards and kanban columns are optional; cleanliness scores are not the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

OPEN_QUESTIONS

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

TEAM

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-023

  2. EL-025

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Can responsible people tell the current and abnormal state without reconstructing it manually?

  2. Are displays fresh, action-linked and non-punitive rather than ceremonial surveillance?

  3. Would additional visual management solve a real ambiguity or merely add another board?

current_evidence_warrant

S005 supports andon history. S066's small review cannot prove all labeling, boards or telemetry effective.

incremental_control_cost

Updating displays and responder attention, privacy and access burden.

EL-004
id

EL-004

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-004

PROPERTY_NAME

Conditional pull/release control and explicit WIP limits

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota supermarket/kanban; operations-science formalisation; software Kanban translation.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Downstream withdrawal and explicit limits prevent premature production and excess work in process.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Unbounded release creates congestion, long feedback, priority churn and hidden defects.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Queue growth, overproduction, premature commitment and excessive context switching.

MECHANISM

Set an explicit bound or release rule for active commitments; admit new work when capacity becomes available; define exceptions and service classes.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Arrival pressure exceeds completion capacity, active work accumulates, or long WIP correlates with delay/quality loss.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For low-volume work with naturally bounded commitments, use a simple finish-before-start norm or periodic capacity check.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Observable WIP boundary; stable item definitions; capacity/replenishment capability; priority and exception policy; no hidden pre-entry queue.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Shorter and more predictable lead time, clearer constraints, faster defect feedback.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Starvation; hidden backlog before entry; arbitrary limits; gaming item size/status; blocking critical long-lead work; apparent cycle-time gains through exclusion.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

A pull signal is not trustworthy demand or capacity; board-level limits can displace queues and invite metric gaming.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Pull was clarified as WIP limitation rather than cards or make-to-order; software translation retained limits but evidence shows optimal limits are contextual.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Use empirical limits by work class, monitor blocked/aged work and total demand, and adapt limits with capacity, variability and service risk.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Kanban cards/boards are not required. A database rule, token, CONWIP, admission policy or finite-capacity scheduler can implement it.

CURRENT_STATUS

RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_MECHANISTIC_MODERATE_EMPIRICAL

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S003

  2. S005

  3. S006

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S032

  2. S033

  3. S039

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S032

  2. S034

  3. S055

  4. S056

OPEN_QUESTIONS

What causal evidence is sufficient to distinguish a useful limit from demand suppression or work reclassification?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

CROSS_FUNCTIONAL

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

Value: congestion and feedback reduction. Assumes a valid boundary and enough response capacity. Over-application causes starvation or displaced queues. Pair with EL-008 and EL-010.

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-007

  2. EL-008

  3. EL-029

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the target system explicitly limit active commitments or merely display them?

  2. Are hidden upstream queues, work-item splitting and exception classes visible?

  3. Does the limit trigger only where congestion is a demonstrated failure mode?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

EL-005
id

EL-005

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-005

PROPERTY_NAME

Batch-size economics and small reversible increments

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

TPS setup reduction and small lots; Lean product development and Continuous Delivery translation.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

HYBRID

ORIGINAL_FORM

Reduce lot size to shorten feedback and lower inventory once setup costs permit.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Large batches delay information, amplify rework and make failures expensive.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Late defect discovery, large release risk, long queue time and irreversible commitment.

MECHANISM

Model batch benefits against setup, validation, coordination and transport costs; reduce setup first; choose a reversible economic batch.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Feedback delay or failure blast radius is material and switching/release cost can be reduced.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

If changes are already tiny or validation has a fixed high cost, keep the economic batch and improve feedback within it.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Measurable setup/release costs; modular work; validation capability; rollback/recovery; ability to integrate increments.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Faster learning, smaller failures, lower inventory and release risk.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

One-piece-flow dogma; fragmentation overhead; excessive coordination; local micro-batches accumulating into system batches; unsafe partial delivery.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Smallness is not value; a minimum batch can be uneconomic, and knowledge work may require coherent experimental units.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Generalised from physical lots to information, decisions and software changes; product development added option preservation rather than indiscriminate decomposition.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Select batch size from total economics and reversibility, not a universal minimum; make delayed feedback and blast radius explicit.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

One-piece flow, daily releases or story slicing are implementations, not the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_MECHANISTIC_MODERATE_EMPIRICAL

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S004

  2. S007

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S032

  2. S036

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S057

  2. S061

  3. S062

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should economic batch size be estimated where quality evidence is sparse or validation cost is nonlinear?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

TEAM

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

Value: shorter feedback and smaller failure scope. Assumes manageable setup/validation. Overuse creates fragmentation and coordination waste.

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-006

  2. EL-029

  3. EL-036

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the system choose batch size from feedback, setup, validation and failure economics?

  2. Can it use a cheap path when smaller batches would add ceremony or integration cost?

  3. Are locally small batches still bundled into a large end-to-end release?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

EL-006
id

EL-006

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-006

PROPERTY_NAME

Setup, changeover, release and feedback latency reduction

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota setup-time reduction and industrial engineering; later software deployment automation.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Lower die-change and process-switching time so mixed production and small lots become economical.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

High transition cost forces large batches, inflexibility and infrequent feedback.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Long feedback loops, large economic batches, downtime and costly recovery.

MECHANISM

Separate necessary from avoidable setup; externalise safe preparation; simplify interfaces; automate repeatable checks; measure total transition and recovery.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Setup, integration, release or environment preparation materially determines batch size, delay or downtime.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

If setup is rare and immaterial, document the path and avoid an optimisation programme.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Stable interfaces; safe automation; competent maintainers; configuration control; rollback and qualification.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Greater flexibility, smaller economic batches, higher availability and faster learning.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Unsafe shortcuts; preparation shifted to another team; brittle automation; ergonomic or quality risk; faster release of low-value work.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Local setup savings often omit preparation, validation and recovery transferred elsewhere.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Extended from physical die changes to software pipelines, environment provisioning, integration and decision-feedback latency.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Reduce total safe transition cost, including preparation, validation, recovery and burden transferred across boundaries.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

SMED workshops, coloured steps and checklists are optional; lower total transition cost is the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

STRONGLY_RETAINED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_CONVERGENT

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S004

  2. S007

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S024

  2. S025

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S032

  2. S057

OPEN_QUESTIONS

Which transition costs are shifted to suppliers, operations or workers and therefore missed by local measures?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

TEAM

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

Value: enables smaller batches and flexible capacity. Assumes quality and safety are preserved. Overuse hides transferred work or brittle automation.

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-013

  2. EL-023

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Is batch size large because setup, release or feedback is expensive, and has that cost been attacked directly?

  2. Does the metric include validation, recovery and transferred work?

  3. Would automation lower total safe transition cost or merely automate a fragile process?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

EL-007
id

EL-007

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-007

PROPERTY_NAME

Demand-capacity pacing and production levelling

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Kiichiro/Ohno JIT, takt and heijunka in repetitive automotive production.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Use demand pace and mix levelling to coordinate repetitive production and reduce unevenness.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Uncoordinated release creates surges, starvation, overtime and supplier amplification.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Demand/capacity mismatch, violent mix changes, uneven workloads and hidden overburden.

MECHANISM

Estimate sustainable pace by work class; level the predictable component; explicitly buffer residual variation; replan when assumptions break.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Repeatable work, measurable aggregate demand, flexible capacity and feasible mix sequencing.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For unique, exploratory or very low-volume work, use finite-capacity planning, rolling forecasts and explicit priority rather than takt ceremony.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Demand information; cross-trained/flexible resources; setup capability; exception policy; workload and ergonomic safeguards; buffers.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Smoother flow, less oscillation, clearer overload and more stable supplier/capacity planning.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

False precision; backlog or overtime used to hide variation; forced pace and work intensification; brittle high-mix schedules; supplier burden transfer.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Takt can become a coercive pace standard; conventional levelling has weak applicability in high-mix/low-volume or discovery work.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Later research narrows conventional levelling to stable/repetitive contexts and develops HMLV adaptations; resilience hybrids preserve reserves.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Level only forecastable aggregate demand, maintain capacity/time buffers and protect workload limits; treat takt as diagnostic, not a metronome.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Takt boards, heijunka boxes and fixed pitch are manufacturing implementations, not general requirements.

CURRENT_STATUS

CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

MODERATE_CONTEXTUAL

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S002

  2. S004

  3. S005

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S025

  2. S026

  3. S064

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S034

  2. S063

  3. S064

OPEN_QUESTIONS

Robust rules for levelling highly variable knowledge work remain unresolved; evidence is dominated by manufacturing cases.

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

SYSTEM_OWNER

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

Value: reduces oscillation and overload. Assumes repeatability and demand aggregation. Overuse converts variability into backlog, overtime or supplier stress.

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-008

  2. EL-023

  3. EL-029

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Is the work repetitive and demand sufficiently observable for a pace or levelling rule to be meaningful?

  2. Where does residual variation go—capacity reserve, inventory, delay, overtime or suppliers?

  3. Does the mechanism reveal overload or impose an unsafe pace?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

EL-008
id

EL-008

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-008

PROPERTY_NAME

Explicit buffer portfolio and strategic slack

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Operations-science refinement of JIT; Toyota capacity practices; post-disruption resilience research.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

REACTION_TO_LEAN

ORIGINAL_FORM

JIT sought minimum necessary stock while Toyota also used capacity and time arrangements to absorb variability.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Removing one buffer silently transfers variability and disruption cost to another part of the system.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Fragility, starvation, burnout, missed service, recovery delay and hidden risk transfer.

MECHANISM

Model variability, lead times, failure consequence and recovery; assign each inventory, capacity, time, information or redundancy buffer a purpose, owner, activation trigger, size logic and review date.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Variability or disruption cannot be eliminated economically and service or failure consequences are material.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For low-risk, rapidly recoverable work, keep only a small contingency and explicit recovery path.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Demand/failure data; risk appetite; cross-boundary cost visibility; authority to hold reserve; anti-hoarding review.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Lower expected total cost, better recovery, less emergency expediting and reduced human overburden.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Slack becomes unmanaged delay; reserves are raided for utilisation; inventory obsolescence; redundancy hides defects; false quantitative precision.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Classic Lean rhetoric often treats inventory and slack as waste, while disruption and inventory-performance evidence shows non-monotonic returns.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

JIT absolutism was replaced by minimal total buffering cost and segment-specific JIT/JIC/resilience designs.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Optimise a portfolio of justified buffers; distinguish protective reserve from accidental queue and continuously reassess assumptions.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Safety stock, spare capacity, backup suppliers or time contingencies are implementations. No universal buffer is mandatory.

CURRENT_STATUS

STRONGLY_RETAINED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_CONVERGENT

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S002

  2. S006

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S033

  2. S039

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S032

  2. S034

  3. S040

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should correlated and deep-tier risks be priced when probabilities are poorly known?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

SYSTEM_OWNER

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

Core resilience property. Value: absorbs unavoidable variability. Overuse creates cost/latency; underuse creates fragility.

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-004

  2. EL-007

  3. EL-009

  4. EL-034

  5. EL-035

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the system explicitly choose among inventory, capacity, time, information and redundancy buffers?

  2. Can it distinguish strategic slack from accidental queueing?

  3. Would proposed WIP or inventory reduction merely transfer risk or workload elsewhere?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

qualified_current_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-008

PROPERTY_NAME

Explicit buffer portfolio and strategic slack

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Operations-science refinement of JIT; Toyota capacity practices; post-disruption resilience research.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

REACTION_TO_LEAN

ORIGINAL_FORM

JIT sought minimum necessary stock while Toyota also used capacity and time arrangements to absorb variability.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Removing one buffer silently transfers variability and disruption cost to another part of the system.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Fragility, starvation, burnout, missed service, recovery delay and hidden risk transfer.

MECHANISM

Distinguish physical inventory, capacity and time buffers from enabling information, alternate designs/sources and recovery options. Model variability, lead time, consequence and feasible recovery; assign proportionate purpose, sizing, ownership and change triggers.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Variability or disruption cannot be eliminated economically and service or failure consequences are material.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For low-risk, rapidly recoverable work, keep only a small contingency and explicit recovery path.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Demand/failure data; risk appetite; cross-boundary cost visibility; authority to hold reserve; anti-hoarding review.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Lower expected total cost, better recovery, less emergency expediting and reduced human overburden.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Slack becomes unmanaged delay; reserves are raided for utilisation; inventory obsolescence; redundancy hides defects; false quantitative precision.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Classic Lean rhetoric often treats inventory and slack as waste, while disruption and inventory-performance evidence shows non-monotonic returns.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Later operations analyses and resilience proposals challenge inventory absolutism and support contingent buffer choices. They do not establish that the whole Lean field replaced one doctrine with a single settled portfolio method.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Optimise a portfolio of justified buffers; distinguish protective reserve from accidental queue and continuously reassess assumptions.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Safety stock, spare capacity, backup suppliers or time contingencies are implementations. No universal buffer is mandatory.

bearer

A system's portfolio of physical buffers and resilience-enabling options

scientific_origin

Operations-science model plus analytical and supply-chain interpretation

current_evidence_warrant

EL-009
id

EL-009

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-009

PROPERTY_NAME

Flow and queue-aware capacity over maximum local utilisation

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

TPS attack on overproduction and interprocess queues; queueing/operations-science refinement.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING

ORIGINAL_FORM

Prevent one process from producing work the next process cannot absorb.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

High utilisation under variability creates nonlinear queues and blocks the system constraint.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Congestion, priority churn, long lead time and local output that cannot be absorbed downstream.

MECHANISM

Measure queue age/size and end-to-end throughput; reserve response capacity; subordinate nonconstraints; stop releasing work that worsens congestion.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Queues grow despite high activity or local productivity gains fail to improve total outcome.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For low variability and ample capacity, simple utilisation monitoring plus exception handling is enough.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Valid system boundary; capacity/arrival data; authority across silos; protected slack; outcome measures.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Lower congestion, shorter lead time, better service and less emergency work.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Underutilisation without purpose; politically contested spare capacity; local teams penalised for system choices; bottleneck migration.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Lean deployments may still pursue labour or machine utilisation and headcount reduction despite flow doctrine.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Anti-overproduction was strengthened by queueing theory and later flow metrics, while resilience research legitimised spare capacity.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Optimise end-to-end service and total buffering cost; treat utilisation as a constraint or signal, not a target to maximise.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Cell layouts or line-balancing charts are not required; queue-aware admission and capacity rules are alternatives.

CURRENT_STATUS

STRONGLY_RETAINED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_CONVERGENT

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S003

  2. S005

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S024

  2. S025

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S032

  2. S056

  3. S062

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should public or safety-critical systems value reserve capacity whose benefit appears only during peaks?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

SYSTEM_OWNER

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

Value: avoids congestion collapse. Assumes a meaningful service boundary. Over-application leaves costly idle capacity; under-application creates nonlinear queues.

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-008

  2. EL-035

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the target system optimise end-to-end throughput and latency or reward local busy time and output?

  2. Is reserve capacity protected where variability makes high utilisation dangerous?

  3. Are queue costs and the location of the constraint visible?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

property_id

bearer

A loading policy for a service or production system

scientific_origin

Queueing/flow reasoning, not a universal anti-utilization rule

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-010

PROPERTY_NAME

Demand and replenishment information integrity

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota downstream withdrawal/kanban and supplier coordination; later digital flow systems.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Use downstream consumption to signal upstream replenishment.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Pull based on stale, distorted or gameable signals amplifies shortages and excess.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

False demand, bullwhip, duplicate work, priority gaming and long-lead omissions.

MECHANISM

Define signal source, semantics, freshness, authority, forecast complement and exception path; reconcile consumption with long-lead and capacity data.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Replenishment or release decisions rely on demand signals across time or organisational boundaries.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For direct low-volume requests, use explicit confirmation and a simple backlog.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Trusted item identity; data quality; latency bounds; incentives that do not reward signal manipulation; long-lead visibility.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

More accurate release, fewer shortages/excesses and better capacity planning.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Demand is endogenous or manipulated; urgent work bypasses signals; forecasts ignored; local pull cannot prepare long-lead components.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

A visible pull signal does not establish trustworthy demand or adequate replenishment capability.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Physical kanban became digital replenishment and admission policies; mature form combines actual consumption with forecast, risk and capacity.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A governed demand-information contract, not blind reaction to the latest request.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Physical cards, supermarkets and board movements are optional implementations.

CURRENT_STATUS

RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_MECHANISTIC_MODERATE_EMPIRICAL

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S002

  2. S003

  3. S005

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S039

  2. S040

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S032

  2. S034

  3. S035

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should pull incorporate sparse catastrophic demand and strategic long-lead preparation?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

CROSS_FUNCTIONAL

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

Value: truthful release and replenishment. Assumes timely signals and response capability. Failure creates starvation, oscillation or false urgency.

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-007

  2. EL-008

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Is the demand or pull signal timely, semantically clear and resistant to gaming?

  2. Does the system complement consumption with long-lead, capacity and risk information?

  3. Can urgent exceptions be distinguished from habitual bypass?

EL-011

EL-011

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-011

PROPERTY_NAME

Abnormality detection and signalling

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyoda loom stop antecedent; Toyota andon and visual control.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Detect an abnormal condition at the source and make it visible to the responsible responder.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Defects, unsafe states and process failures can continue silently until downstream discovery.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Silent propagation, delayed response and normalisation of deviation.

MECHANISM

Define detectable abnormal states and confidence; instrument or enable human recognition; signal with severity, location, owner and time.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Continued operation after an abnormality materially increases harm, rework, delay or uncertainty.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For rare, low-consequence events, use a manual report with clear ownership rather than continuous instrumentation.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Known normal envelope; usable detectors; low-friction human signalling; response ownership; alarm review.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Earlier containment and shorter time to response.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

False alarms, missed detections, alarm fatigue, ambiguous ownership, suppressed signals and throughput pressure.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Detection without response creates theatre; automated detection can encode an incomplete model and workers may be punished for signalling.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Extended from mechanical stops and lights to telemetry, alerts and operator calls; mature form manages precision, severity and responder load.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Detect and signal decision-relevant abnormalities with calibrated severity, accountable response and feedback on false positives/negatives.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

An andon cord or light is optional; alerts, checks, guardrails or human escalation can implement the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

STRONGLY_RETAINED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_CONVERGENT

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S001

  2. S004

  3. S005

  4. S067

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S025

  2. S029

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S031

  2. S067

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should detection thresholds be tuned when misses are catastrophic but false alarms erode response capacity?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

WORKER_LOCAL

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-012

  2. EL-023

  3. EL-025

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the system expose material abnormalities early enough to change the outcome?

  2. Are detection accuracy, alarm burden and ownership monitored?

  3. Can a person signal safely when automated detection is absent or wrong?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

EL-012

EL-012

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-012

PROPERTY_NAME

Bounded stop, containment, escalation and recovery authority

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Jidoka, fixed-position stopping and andon response in Toyota production.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Stop or hold work when an abnormality cannot be resolved within the local cycle.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

A recognised defect or unsafe condition continues because throughput pressure overrides containment.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Defect propagation, unsafe continuation and unmanaged emergency escalation.

MECHANISM

Define severity-based authority to hold, stop, isolate or degrade service; summon support; specify restart/recovery criteria and appeal.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Expected propagation harm exceeds interruption and recovery cost, or safety/quality boundaries are breached.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For low-consequence reversible anomalies, flag and route to a bounded follow-up queue without stopping the whole system.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Psychological/employment safety; available responders; isolation capability; restart authority; false-stop review; recovery path.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Smaller blast radius, faster expert response, safer operation and preserved evidence.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

False stops, alarm fatigue, punitive culture, ignored calls, local stop causing larger system harm, rapid reset without diagnosis.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

A cord does not confer real authority; line pressure can suppress use, and indiscriminate stopping is costly.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From line stop to tiered containment, circuit breakers, feature flags, quarantine and controlled degradation.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Severity-calibrated, non-punitive stop/containment authority with response time, restart criteria, evidence preservation and post-event learning.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A physical cord, red light or universal stop is not required; bounded isolation and escalation are the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_HISTORICAL_MECHANISTIC

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S004

  2. S005

  3. S006

  4. S067

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S025

  2. S029

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S031

OPEN_QUESTIONS

Direct comparative evidence on stop-authority effectiveness under real production pressure and false alarms remains limited.

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

WORKER_LOCAL

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

Trade-off: containment reduces propagation but interrupts flow. Trigger by severity and expected loss; cheap path is flag-and-follow-up for low consequence.

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-009

  2. EL-023

  3. EL-026

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the system give abnormalities a bounded stop, containment or escalation path rather than merely record them?

  2. Who may stop, who must respond, and who may restart?

  3. Does throughput pressure or punishment make the nominal authority unusable?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

qualified_current_record

bearer

EL-013

EL-013

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-013

PROPERTY_NAME

Quality at source and defect containment

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Jidoka, Toyota SQC/TQC and in-process quality assurance.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Do not pass known defective work to the next process; build checks and correction close to creation.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

End inspection discovers defects after additional work and obscures causal context.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Downstream rework, defect multiplication, ambiguous ownership and late customer harm.

MECHANISM

Place proportionate checks near creation; isolate failed output; preserve trace/evidence; repair or reject under defined criteria.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Defects become more costly or less diagnosable downstream, and source checks have useful sensitivity.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For very low-risk reversible output, use sampled or downstream validation with traceability.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Testability; clear quality criteria; containment path; no incentive to hide defects; qualified repair/release.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Smaller defect escape, faster feedback and less downstream rework.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Over-testing; brittle checks; test gaming; false confidence; quality burden shifted to operators; local pass misses system failure.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Quality at source can become blame at source; inspection does not equal process capability or causal prevention.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Expanded from physical inspection and stop to automated tests, contracts, validation gates and canary release, paired with recovery.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Use risk-based source verification and containment with independent system-level assurance where local checks cannot see emergent failure.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Inspection stamps, quality gates or test suites are implementations; early trustworthy containment is the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

STRONGLY_RETAINED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_CONVERGENT

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S001

  2. S005

  3. S010

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S046

  2. S060

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S041

  2. S057

  3. S061

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should source checks be balanced against independent assurance for systemic or adversarial failures?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

WORKER_LOCAL

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-005

  2. EL-006

  3. EL-009

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Are defects detected and contained before avoidable downstream propagation?

  2. Does a local pass actually establish system quality, or is independent assurance needed?

  3. Can workers report defects without absorbing blame for structural causes?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

qualified_current_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-013

PROPERTY_NAME

Quality at source and defect containment

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Jidoka, Toyota SQC/TQC and in-process quality assurance.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Do not pass known defective work to the next process; build checks and correction close to creation.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

End inspection discovers defects after additional work and obscures causal context.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Downstream rework, defect multiplication, ambiguous ownership and late customer harm.

MECHANISM

Place proportionate checks near creation; isolate failed output; preserve trace/evidence; repair or reject under defined criteria.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Defects become more costly or less diagnosable downstream, and source checks have useful sensitivity.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For very low-risk reversible output, use sampled or downstream validation with traceability.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Testability; clear quality criteria; containment path; no incentive to hide defects; qualified repair/release.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Smaller defect escape, faster feedback and less downstream rework.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Over-testing; brittle checks; test gaming; false confidence; quality burden shifted to operators; local pass misses system failure.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Quality at source can become blame at source; inspection does not equal process capability or causal prevention.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Historical source-quality checks and modern software tests/validation address related propagation risks. The latter are domain translations or convergences, not established direct lineage.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Use risk-based source verification and containment with independent system-level assurance where local checks cannot see emergent failure.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Inspection stamps, quality gates or test suites are implementations; early trustworthy containment is the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

STRONGLY_RETAINED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_CONVERGENT

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S001

  2. S005

  3. S010

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S046

  2. S060

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S041

  2. S057

  3. S061

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should source checks be balanced against independent assurance for systemic or adversarial failures?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

WORKER_LOCAL

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-005

  2. EL-006

  3. EL-009

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Are defects detected and contained before avoidable downstream propagation?

  2. Does a local pass actually establish system quality, or is independent assurance needed?

  3. Can workers report defects without absorbing blame for structural causes?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

REQUALIFICATION

S001/S005/S010 support source quality logic. Local checks cannot establish unseen interface or system-level properties.

incremental_control_cost

Inspection, validation delays and missed-mode risk.

qualitative_evaluation_criterion

Test the relevant failure before propagation when feasible, preserving additional system assurance where local scope is insufficient.

EL-014

EL-014

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-014

PROPERTY_NAME

Mistake-proofing and fail-safe constraints

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota/Shingo-associated poka-yoke and built-in quality; broader human-factors convergence.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Design fixtures, sequences or sensors so predictable errors are prevented or immediately exposed.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Known recurrent slips remain possible and rely on vigilance or final inspection.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Repeatable human error, wrong-part use, omitted steps and unsafe state transitions.

MECHANISM

Eliminate the error opportunity, constrain invalid states, make the correct action easier, or detect the mistake before propagation.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Error mode is recurrent, well understood and cheaply preventable or detectable.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Use a clear cue or checklist for infrequent low-consequence tasks rather than hard constraint.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Accurate error model; escape/override for legitimate exceptions; human-factors testing; maintenance.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Lower defect frequency and reduced dependence on memory or vigilance.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Brittle constraints; workarounds; blocking legitimate variation; new failure modes; ignored sensor faults; operator deskilling.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

A device can freeze an incorrect process model and shift error elsewhere; not all complex failure has a local mistake.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From physical jigs and limit switches to type systems, validation, interlocks and safe defaults.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Risk-based constraint or detection for stable error modes, with tested override, monitoring and revision.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A branded poka-yoke device is unnecessary; any verified fail-safe design may implement the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_MECHANISTIC_MODERATE_EMPIRICAL

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S004

  2. S005

  3. S067

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S045

  2. S046

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S041

  2. S057

OPEN_QUESTIONS

Evidence comparing hard prevention, detection and training across complex socio-technical errors is incomplete.

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

TEAM

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-017

  2. EL-022

  3. EL-029

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Is there a recurrent, well-understood error mode that design can remove or expose?

  2. Does the constraint preserve legitimate exceptions and fail safely?

  3. Would a hard control solve the cause or merely force workarounds?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

EL-015

EL-015

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-015

PROPERTY_NAME

Jidoka/autonomation: automate routine operation while reserving human exception judgement

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyoda automatic loom and Toyota machine autonomation.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Machines operate without constant watching and stop when an abnormality occurs so one person can oversee several.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Humans are consumed by routine monitoring while automation continues producing bad output.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Unattended defect propagation and wasteful human machine-watching.

MECHANISM

Automate stable routine execution and detection; stop or hand off uncertain/abnormal cases with context to a competent human.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Routine is stable enough to automate, abnormalities are detectable, and human attention is scarce or high-value.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Keep manual work where volume is low, judgement dominates or automation cannot fail safely.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Reliable detection; safe state; human response capacity; understandable handoff; maintenance; workload monitoring.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Higher leverage of human attention, earlier containment and lower repetitive monitoring burden.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Automation bias; deskilling; opaque models; alert overload; unsafe autonomous continuation; humans left with only rare hard cases.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Automation may be justified as labour reduction and can worsen surveillance, pace or job quality; 'human touch' rhetoric is not sufficient.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Modern form resembles human-on-the-loop automation with bounded autonomy, observability and graceful degradation.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Automate only stable, testable routine work; make uncertainty and abnormality explicit; preserve human competence and safe fallback.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Toyota-style machines, andon lights or robots are not required; bounded human-machine allocation is the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_HISTORICAL_MODERATE_TRANSFER

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S001

  2. S005

  3. S067

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S025

  2. S026

  3. S029

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S041

  2. S057

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should organisations preserve human competence when automation makes intervention rare but consequential?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

SYSTEM_OWNER

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-021

  2. EL-023

  3. EL-026

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Is automation limited to stable routine work with safe exception handoff?

  2. Can humans understand, override and recover the system?

  3. Does the automation reduce harmful work or merely reduce headcount and concentrate cognitive risk?

EL-016

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-016

PROPERTY_NAME

Process capability, variation measurement and statistical quality control

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Shared Shewhart/Deming statistical-quality ancestry; Toyota SQC from 1949 and TQC from 1961.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

SHARED_QUALITY_ANCESTRY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Use control charts and statistical methods to distinguish process behaviour, pursue causes and prevent recurrence.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Anecdotal reactions to variation confuse noise with assignable causes and fail to establish capability.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Tampering, recurrent defects, false improvement and unstable output.

MECHANISM

Define measures and sampling; examine stability and capability; distinguish common/special causes; test countermeasures over time.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Repeated measurable output where variation matters and observations are sufficiently comparable.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For rare or unique work, use structured case evidence and direct tests rather than force a control chart.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Measurement-system validity; enough observations; statistical competence; stable definitions; authority to change systemic causes.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Earlier instability detection, better causal discrimination and qualified process improvement.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Chart ritual; bad data; arbitrary limits; metric gaming; treating statistical control as fitness for use; ignoring rare catastrophic modes.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Lean accounts sometimes appropriate quality ancestry; Deming did not originate JIT or jidoka, and exact influence on A3/5 Whys is unproven.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Expanded from shop-floor control charts to reliability, experimentation and longitudinal outcome evidence.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Use fit-for-purpose variation analysis and capability evidence, explicitly separating stability, customer need and risk.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A control-chart poster, Deming Prize programme or Six Sigma belt is not required.

CURRENT_STATUS

STRONGLY_RETAINED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_HISTORICAL_EMPIRICAL

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S010

  2. S011

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S045

  2. S047

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S041

  2. S042

  3. S062

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should sparse, nonstationary and dependent observations be governed without false statistical certainty?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

CROSS_FUNCTIONAL

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-018

  2. EL-025

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the target distinguish signal from routine variation before changing the process?

  2. Are measurements valid and linked to fitness for use rather than merely stable?

  3. Would statistical machinery add value for this population or create false precision?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

current_evidence_warrant

S010/S011 support institutional adoption; they do not prove that any chosen chart is valid or that stable output meets user needs.

incremental_control_cost

Sampling, measurement assurance and analytical expertise.

qualitative_evaluation_criterion

Specify data and assumptions; separate stability, capability and acceptability. No theorem is asserted from a chart label.

EL-017

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-017

PROPERTY_NAME

Explicit current baseline with controlled revision

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota standardized work (takt, sequence, standard WIP); TWI Job Instruction; later learning-system interpretation.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Specify the current work sequence, pace and standard inventory so it can be taught and managed.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Implicit practice makes deviation, training and improvement impossible to evaluate.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Uncontrolled variation, knowledge loss, untestable change and inconsistent training.

MECHANISM

Version the best-known current method, scope, expected result and exceptions; train it; expose deviations; test and authorise revisions.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Work recurs, consistency matters, multiple people perform it or failure consequences require a known baseline.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For one-off low-risk work, retain a short decision record or checklist rather than a full standard-work sheet.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Worker input; valid current evidence; change and exception authority; review owner/date; ergonomic and safety assessment.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Repeatability, faster training, visible deviation and a platform for improvement.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Immutable procedure; stale paperwork; coercive pace; deskilling; suppressed expert judgement; standards written by distant staff; unauthorised workarounds.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Labour studies show formalisation can reduce autonomy and wellbeing; Toyota doctrine does not prove democratic control.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Reinterpreted from prescribed sequence toward an explicit, testable, provisional baseline with lifecycle controls.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A scoped, versioned, teachable baseline with exceptions, evidence, local challenge rights, qualification, expiry and retirement.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A standard-work sheet is optional. Executable checks, runbooks, contracts or annotated examples can serve.

CURRENT_STATUS

RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_HISTORICAL_MIXED_SOCIAL

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S004

  2. S008

  3. S009

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S025

  2. S026

  3. S028

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S020

  2. S031

  3. S041

OPEN_QUESTIONS

Historical evidence for explicit expiry/retirement inside Toyota is limited; those safeguards are evolved engineering additions.

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-014

  2. EL-022

  3. EL-029

  4. EL-040

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the system distinguish a current baseline from an immutable rule?

  2. Who may deviate, propose revision, qualify the change and retire the standard?

  3. Is the baseline still valid, usable and safe rather than paperwork compliance?

EL-018

EL-018

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-018

PROPERTY_NAME

Direct observation combined with traceable system evidence

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota genchi genbutsu; quality-control measurement; modern observability convergence.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Go to the actual place, see the actual work and grasp facts before deciding.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Reports and abstractions can hide real conditions, while local impressions can also misrepresent distributed systems.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Decision by hearsay, dashboard-only management, executive theatre and local anecdotal causation.

MECHANISM

Observe work and users directly, then triangulate with traces, records, samples and affected-worker testimony; preserve provenance.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Material uncertainty exists about what actually happens or reports conflict with outcomes.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For well-instrumented, low-risk routine work, sample direct observation periodically and investigate exceptions.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Access; non-punitive observation; representative sampling; traceability; skill in distinguishing observation from inference.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Better problem framing, discovery of unrecorded work and stronger causal hypotheses.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Gemba walks as theatre; surveillance; observer effects; anecdotal overreach; privileging visible local work over invisible dependencies.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Direct seeing is neither sufficient nor neutral; distributed software, supply networks and latent harms require instrumentation and testimony.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Mature form treats direct observation as one evidence channel in a provenance-bearing triangulation.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Triangulate actual-work observation, system traces, outcome data and stakeholder accounts; label inference and uncertainty.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A scheduled gemba walk is not required; contextual inquiry, trace review or incident reconstruction may be better.

CURRENT_STATUS

RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

MODERATE_CONVERGENT

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S010

  2. S068

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S029

  2. S060

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S020

  2. S031

  3. S061

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How much observation frequency is enough under rapid change, privacy constraints and distributed work?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

TEAM

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-016

  2. EL-023

  3. EL-025

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does the system obtain evidence from actual work rather than reports alone?

  2. Is direct observation triangulated with traces, outcomes and affected people's testimony?

  3. Could the observation process distort work or become surveillance?

EL-019

EL-019

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-019

PROPERTY_NAME

Evidence-bound problem solving and countermeasure qualification

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota kaizen/problem solving, SQC/TQC, Ohno's Five Whys; Shewhart/Deming PDSA ancestry.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

HYBRID

ORIGINAL_FORM

Investigate an abnormality, ask why, try a countermeasure and standardise successful change.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Symptoms recur because fixes are selected without causal evidence or tested only once.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Template-driven action, single-root stories, recurrence and false learning.

MECHANISM

Define problem and baseline; gather evidence; develop competing causal hypotheses; predict effects; test safely; compare results; check side effects and recurrence.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

The failure is material, recurrent, causally uncertain or costly enough to justify investigation.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For a trivial one-off reversible issue, fix and record the observation; escalate only on recurrence.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Time and skill; data/provenance; ability to test; independent challenge; authority over causes; follow-up owner.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Fewer recurrences, stronger learning and countermeasures that survive outside the initial event.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

A3/5-Whys ritual; confirmation bias; singular root cause; weak controls; no prediction; post hoc success; political exclusion of structural causes.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Format-specific causal evidence is weak; reviews show PDSA often used without core features and RCA often produces weak actions.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Moved from named templates toward explicit scientific reasoning, systems causation and countermeasure qualification.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A proportional causal-learning loop with competing hypotheses, predictions, safe tests, outcome/side-effect evidence and recurrence review.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A3 paper, exactly five whys, fishbone or PDCA labels are optional prompts and never evidence.

CURRENT_STATUS

STRONGLY_RETAINED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH_CONVERGENT_WEAK_FORMAT_SPECIFIC

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S007

  2. S010

  3. S012

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S045

  2. S046

  3. S048

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S043

  2. S047

OPEN_QUESTIONS

Which problem classes justify experimentation, formal causal analysis or independent review remains context-dependent.

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-020

  2. EL-024

  3. EL-038

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Does repeated failure produce competing causal hypotheses and a qualified countermeasure rather than a completed template?

  2. Are predictions, disconfirming evidence, side effects and recurrence checked?

  3. Would a cheap fix suffice, or is the causal uncertainty consequential enough to trigger the full loop?

S046/S047/S048 support concern about ritual and weak inquiry; S047 does not show that reported PDSA fidelity causes success. A3/Five Whys names confer no evidential force.

incremental_control_cost

Investigation, safe testing, alternatives and follow-up.

qualitative_evaluation_criterion

A prediction is compared with observed results and competing explanations at proportionate scope; a valid result may be no change.

  1. C04

  2. C10

  3. C11

EL-020
id

EL-020

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-020

PROPERTY_NAME

Improvement sustainment, expiry, retirement and economics

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Kaizen follow-up, standardisation after improvement; later sustainment and deimplementation research.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

LEAN_PRODUCTION_GENERALISATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Standardise a successful improvement so gains persist.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Changes decay, create maintenance burden, outlive their premise or continue after value disappears.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Improvement theatre, regression, zombie controls and unbounded process complexity.

MECHANISM

Assign owner, expected benefit, maintenance cost, verification interval, expiry/renewal rule, rollback and retirement evidence.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

A countermeasure changes recurring work or introduces continuing control/maintenance cost.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For ephemeral low-risk changes, define a short observation period and automatic expiry.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Baseline and outcome data; ownership; ability to remove controls; preserved rationale; leadership continuity.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Durable gains, lower bureaucracy, timely removal of obsolete controls and transparent improvement economics.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Audit-only sustainment; permanent controls after risk changes; sunk-cost defence; leadership turnover; benefits not remeasured.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Kaizen-event studies show human outcomes and gains require follow-up; local improvements often lack customer or system outcome evidence.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Added lifecycle, deimplementation and maintenance economics to 'standardise and spread'.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Every material improvement is a governed hypothesis with benefit, burden, review, expiry and retirement path.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Follow-up audits or control plans are implementations; evidence-bearing lifecycle ownership is the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

MODERATE_EMPIRICAL

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S020

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S046

  2. S048

  3. S060

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S043

  2. S044

  3. S061

OPEN_QUESTIONS

Long-term comparative evidence on retirement and deimplementation inside Lean programmes is limited.

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

SYSTEM_OWNER

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-017

  2. EL-038

  3. EL-040

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Who owns the improvement after launch, and when is benefit rechecked?

  2. Can obsolete standards, controls and metrics expire or be retired?

  3. Does maintenance cost exceed the current value of the improvement?

EL-021
id

EL-021

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-021

PROPERTY_NAME

Worker capability, training and cross-skilling

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota supervisor/operator development, TWI Job Instruction, standard work and quality-circle traditions.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Train workers and supervisors to perform, recognise abnormality and improve the work.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

A tightly coupled system fails when knowledge is tacit, narrow or unavailable at the point of need.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Skill bottlenecks, unsafe improvisation, slow recovery and dependence on a few experts.

MECHANISM

Define required capabilities; train through demonstration and practice; verify competence; cross-skill for response; preserve coaching time.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Work recurs, failures require local response, or capacity flexibility depends on transferable skill.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For rare low-risk work, retain accessible guidance and an expert escalation path rather than broad certification.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Paid training time; qualified instructors; humane workload; current baselines; opportunities to practise; recognition of expertise.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Safer execution, flexible capacity, faster abnormality response and more credible local improvement.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Training as compliance; skill matrices without ability; unpaid improvement burden; multiskilling used only to cut staffing; obsolete instruction.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Participation and cross-skilling can intensify work and widen responsibility without control, pay or security.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From job instruction and narrow shop skills toward socio-technical capability, coaching, recovery practice and knowledge continuity.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Maintain verified capability and cross-coverage for material work, paired with authority, workload protection and fair recognition.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

TWI cards, dojos and skill matrices are optional; demonstrated capability and coverage are the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

SOCIO_TECHNICAL_CONTEXT_PROPERTY

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

MODERATE_HISTORICAL_MIXED_EMPIRICAL

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S006

  2. S008

  3. S009

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S025

  2. S026

  3. S028

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S031

  2. S041

  3. S070

OPEN_QUESTIONS

Evidence separating training benefit from selection, staffing and management quality is incomplete.

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

TEAM

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-015

  2. EL-023

  3. EL-035

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Do people have trained capability to execute, detect abnormality, recover and improve—not just responsibility?

  2. Is cross-skilling supported by time, pay and staffing rather than used as headcount extraction?

  3. Is competence verified and refreshed when the baseline changes?

DISPOSITION_NOTE

EL-022
id

EL-022

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-022

PROPERTY_NAME

Safe voice, participation and bounded change authority

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota respect-for-human doctrine, suggestion systems, quality circles, andon and NUMMI involvement.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Workers participate in operating and improving the workshop and can call attention to abnormalities.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

People closest to work possess information but cannot safely expose or change problems.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Suppressed alarms, learned helplessness, management blindness and pseudo-participation.

MECHANISM

Provide non-punitive signal, response and appeal channels; specify local/team/cross-functional change rights; protect dissent and stop authority.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Front-line observation is necessary for quality, safety or improvement and management cannot centrally see conditions.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For simple low-risk work, a direct owner contact and documented response is enough.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Employment/psychological safety; response obligation; workload and time; representation; transparent decisions; escalation above local management.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Earlier problem exposure, richer evidence, faster local correction and greater legitimacy.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Suggestion quotas; ignored andons; consultation without decision rights; retaliation; responsibility without resources; surveillance.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Toyota and Lean rhetoric often labels involvement 'empowerment' while pace, staffing and strategic decisions remain managerial.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Mature form distinguishes voice, information, consent, local change authority, stop authority and bargaining power.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A governed rights-and-response system: safe signal, bounded local action, appeal, documented disposition and protection against workload transfer.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Suggestion boxes, quality circles and daily huddles are optional; real voice and decision rights are the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

SOCIO_TECHNICAL_CONTEXT_PROPERTY

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

MIXED_EMPIRICAL_HIGH_NORMATIVE

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S006

  2. S013

  3. S015

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S025

  2. S026

  3. S029

EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE

  1. S027

  2. S028

  3. S031

OPEN_QUESTIONS

Which governance arrangements reliably preserve voice under cost and schedule pressure across labour regimes?

IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY

CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE

NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY

POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY

  1. EL-007

  2. EL-012

  3. EL-017

  4. EL-023

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Can workers safely signal, stop, challenge and propose change, and must the organisation respond?

  2. What decisions are worker-local, team, supervisory, cross-functional or system-owner?

  3. Does participation change decisions or merely supply unpaid improvement labour?

EL-023
id

EL-023

historical_native_record
PROPERTY_ID

EL-023

PROPERTY_NAME

Employment security, trust and fair distribution of productivity gains

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Toyota's post-1950 labour compact and later joint declarations; NUMMI employment-security practice.

LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION

SOCIO_TECHNICAL_CONTEXT_PROPERTY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Link productivity improvement with stable employment, working conditions and labour-management trust.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Workers rationally conceal improvement opportunities when improvement threatens jobs, workload or bargaining position.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Trust destruction, sabotage, hidden problems, fear-based compliance and 'lean and mean' layoffs.

MECHANISM

Set credible no-layoff or redeployment rules for improvement gains where feasible; negotiate workload; share gains; protect reporting and transition.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Improvement solicits worker knowledge, reduces labour content or changes job design materially.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For small changes with no employment/workload effect, state that boundary and monitor cumulative effects.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Credible management commitment; financial capacity; worker representation/voice; redeployment and training; transparent gain accounting.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

More truthful problem exposure, cooperation, capability retention and lower fear.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Promises withdrawn in downturns; gains captured by owners; redeployment becomes intensification; insiders protected while suppliers/contractors absorb risk.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Toyota's 1950 dispute and wider Lean layoffs show security is not inherent; national institutions and firm economics matter.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Moved from implicit Japanese/NUMMI compact to explicit socio-technical prerequisite and fair-transition requirement.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Before labour-saving improvement, define employment, workload, compensation, redeployment and supplier effects; treat trust as evidence-bearing governance.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Respect-for-people slogans, town halls and culture statements do not establish security or fair gains.
