EL-008 — Explicit buffer portfolio and strategic slack
PROPERTY ID
EL-008
PROPERTY NAME
Explicit buffer portfolio and strategic slack
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Operations-science refinement of JIT; Toyota capacity practices; post-disruption resilience research.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
REACTION_TO_LEAN
ORIGINAL FORM
JIT sought minimum necessary stock while Toyota also used capacity and time arrangements to absorb variability.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Removing one buffer silently transfers variability and disruption cost to another part of the system.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Fragility, starvation, burnout, missed service, recovery delay and hidden risk transfer.
MECHANISM
Model variability, lead times, failure consequence and recovery; assign each inventory, capacity, time, information or redundancy buffer a purpose, owner, activation trigger, size logic and review date.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Variability or disruption cannot be eliminated economically and service or failure consequences are material.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For low-risk, rapidly recoverable work, keep only a small contingency and explicit recovery path.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Demand/failure data; risk appetite; cross-boundary cost visibility; authority to hold reserve; anti-hoarding review.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Lower expected total cost, better recovery, less emergency expediting and reduced human overburden.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Slack becomes unmanaged delay; reserves are raided for utilisation; inventory obsolescence; redundancy hides defects; false quantitative precision.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Classic Lean rhetoric often treats inventory and slack as waste, while disruption and inventory-performance evidence shows non-monotonic returns.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
JIT absolutism was replaced by minimal total buffering cost and segment-specific JIT/JIC/resilience designs.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Optimise a portfolio of justified buffers; distinguish protective reserve from accidental queue and continuously reassess assumptions.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Safety stock, spare capacity, backup suppliers or time contingencies are implementations. No universal buffer is mandatory.

  3. Would proposed WIP or inventory reduction merely transfer risk or workload elsewhere?

EL-009 — Flow and queue-aware capacity over maximum local utilisation
PROPERTY ID
EL-009
PROPERTY NAME
Flow and queue-aware capacity over maximum local utilisation
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
TPS attack on overproduction and interprocess queues; queueing/operations-science refinement.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING
ORIGINAL FORM
Prevent one process from producing work the next process cannot absorb.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
High utilisation under variability creates nonlinear queues and blocks the system constraint.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Congestion, priority churn, long lead time and local output that cannot be absorbed downstream.
MECHANISM
Measure queue age/size and end-to-end throughput; reserve response capacity; subordinate nonconstraints; stop releasing work that worsens congestion.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Queues grow despite high activity or local productivity gains fail to improve total outcome.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For low variability and ample capacity, simple utilisation monitoring plus exception handling is enough.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Valid system boundary; capacity/arrival data; authority across silos; protected slack; outcome measures.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Lower congestion, shorter lead time, better service and less emergency work.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Underutilisation without purpose; politically contested spare capacity; local teams penalised for system choices; bottleneck migration.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Lean deployments may still pursue labour or machine utilisation and headcount reduction despite flow doctrine.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Anti-overproduction was strengthened by queueing theory and later flow metrics, while resilience research legitimised spare capacity.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Optimise end-to-end service and total buffering cost; treat utilisation as a constraint or signal, not a target to maximise.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Cell layouts or line-balancing charts are not required; queue-aware admission and capacity rules are alternatives.

EL-010 — Demand and replenishment information integrity
PROPERTY ID
EL-010
PROPERTY NAME
Demand and replenishment information integrity
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota downstream withdrawal/kanban and supplier coordination; later digital flow systems.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Use downstream consumption to signal upstream replenishment.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Pull based on stale, distorted or gameable signals amplifies shortages and excess.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
False demand, bullwhip, duplicate work, priority gaming and long-lead omissions.
MECHANISM
Define signal source, semantics, freshness, authority, forecast complement and exception path; reconcile consumption with long-lead and capacity data.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Replenishment or release decisions rely on demand signals across time or organisational boundaries.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For direct low-volume requests, use explicit confirmation and a simple backlog.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Trusted item identity; data quality; latency bounds; incentives that do not reward signal manipulation; long-lead visibility.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
More accurate release, fewer shortages/excesses and better capacity planning.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Demand is endogenous or manipulated; urgent work bypasses signals; forecasts ignored; local pull cannot prepare long-lead components.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
A visible pull signal does not establish trustworthy demand or adequate replenishment capability.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Physical kanban became digital replenishment and admission policies; mature form combines actual consumption with forecast, risk and capacity.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
A governed demand-information contract, not blind reaction to the latest request.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Physical cards, supermarkets and board movements are optional implementations.

EL-011 — Abnormality detection and signalling
PROPERTY ID
EL-011
PROPERTY NAME
Abnormality detection and signalling
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyoda loom stop antecedent; Toyota andon and visual control.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Detect an abnormal condition at the source and make it visible to the responsible responder.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Defects, unsafe states and process failures can continue silently until downstream discovery.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Silent propagation, delayed response and normalisation of deviation.
MECHANISM
Define detectable abnormal states and confidence; instrument or enable human recognition; signal with severity, location, owner and time.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Continued operation after an abnormality materially increases harm, rework, delay or uncertainty.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For rare, low-consequence events, use a manual report with clear ownership rather than continuous instrumentation.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Known normal envelope; usable detectors; low-friction human signalling; response ownership; alarm review.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Earlier containment and shorter time to response.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
False alarms, missed detections, alarm fatigue, ambiguous ownership, suppressed signals and throughput pressure.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Detection without response creates theatre; automated detection can encode an incomplete model and workers may be punished for signalling.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Extended from mechanical stops and lights to telemetry, alerts and operator calls; mature form manages precision, severity and responder load.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Detect and signal decision-relevant abnormalities with calibrated severity, accountable response and feedback on false positives/negatives.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
An andon cord or light is optional; alerts, checks, guardrails or human escalation can implement the property.

EL-012 — Bounded stop, containment, escalation and recovery authority
PROPERTY ID
EL-012
PROPERTY NAME
Bounded stop, containment, escalation and recovery authority
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Jidoka, fixed-position stopping and andon response in Toyota production.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Stop or hold work when an abnormality cannot be resolved within the local cycle.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
A recognised defect or unsafe condition continues because throughput pressure overrides containment.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Defect propagation, unsafe continuation and unmanaged emergency escalation.
MECHANISM
Define severity-based authority to hold, stop, isolate or degrade service; summon support; specify restart/recovery criteria and appeal.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Expected propagation harm exceeds interruption and recovery cost, or safety/quality boundaries are breached.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For low-consequence reversible anomalies, flag and route to a bounded follow-up queue without stopping the whole system.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Psychological/employment safety; available responders; isolation capability; restart authority; false-stop review; recovery path.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Smaller blast radius, faster expert response, safer operation and preserved evidence.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
False stops, alarm fatigue, punitive culture, ignored calls, local stop causing larger system harm, rapid reset without diagnosis.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
A cord does not confer real authority; line pressure can suppress use, and indiscriminate stopping is costly.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
From line stop to tiered containment, circuit breakers, feature flags, quarantine and controlled degradation.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Severity-calibrated, non-punitive stop/containment authority with response time, restart criteria, evidence preservation and post-event learning.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
A physical cord, red light or universal stop is not required; bounded isolation and escalation are the property.

EL-013 — Quality at source and defect containment
PROPERTY ID
EL-013
PROPERTY NAME
Quality at source and defect containment
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Jidoka, Toyota SQC/TQC and in-process quality assurance.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Do not pass known defective work to the next process; build checks and correction close to creation.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
End inspection discovers defects after additional work and obscures causal context.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Downstream rework, defect multiplication, ambiguous ownership and late customer harm.
MECHANISM
Place proportionate checks near creation; isolate failed output; preserve trace/evidence; repair or reject under defined criteria.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Defects become more costly or less diagnosable downstream, and source checks have useful sensitivity.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For very low-risk reversible output, use sampled or downstream validation with traceability.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Testability; clear quality criteria; containment path; no incentive to hide defects; qualified repair/release.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Smaller defect escape, faster feedback and less downstream rework.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Over-testing; brittle checks; test gaming; false confidence; quality burden shifted to operators; local pass misses system failure.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Quality at source can become blame at source; inspection does not equal process capability or causal prevention.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Expanded from physical inspection and stop to automated tests, contracts, validation gates and canary release, paired with recovery.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Use risk-based source verification and containment with independent system-level assurance where local checks cannot see emergent failure.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Inspection stamps, quality gates or test suites are implementations; early trustworthy containment is the property.

EL-014 — Mistake-proofing and fail-safe constraints
PROPERTY ID
EL-014
PROPERTY NAME
Mistake-proofing and fail-safe constraints
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota/Shingo-associated poka-yoke and built-in quality; broader human-factors convergence.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Design fixtures, sequences or sensors so predictable errors are prevented or immediately exposed.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Known recurrent slips remain possible and rely on vigilance or final inspection.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Repeatable human error, wrong-part use, omitted steps and unsafe state transitions.
MECHANISM
Eliminate the error opportunity, constrain invalid states, make the correct action easier, or detect the mistake before propagation.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Error mode is recurrent, well understood and cheaply preventable or detectable.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
Use a clear cue or checklist for infrequent low-consequence tasks rather than hard constraint.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Accurate error model; escape/override for legitimate exceptions; human-factors testing; maintenance.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Lower defect frequency and reduced dependence on memory or vigilance.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Brittle constraints; workarounds; blocking legitimate variation; new failure modes; ignored sensor faults; operator deskilling.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
A device can freeze an incorrect process model and shift error elsewhere; not all complex failure has a local mistake.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
From physical jigs and limit switches to type systems, validation, interlocks and safe defaults.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Risk-based constraint or detection for stable error modes, with tested override, monitoring and revision.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
A branded poka-yoke device is unnecessary; any verified fail-safe design may implement the property.

EL-015 — Jidoka/autonomation: automate routine operation while reserving human exception judgement
PROPERTY ID
EL-015
PROPERTY NAME
Jidoka/autonomation: automate routine operation while reserving human exception judgement
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyoda automatic loom and Toyota machine autonomation.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Machines operate without constant watching and stop when an abnormality occurs so one person can oversee several.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Humans are consumed by routine monitoring while automation continues producing bad output.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Unattended defect propagation and wasteful human machine-watching.
MECHANISM
Automate stable routine execution and detection; stop or hand off uncertain/abnormal cases with context to a competent human.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Routine is stable enough to automate, abnormalities are detectable, and human attention is scarce or high-value.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
Keep manual work where volume is low, judgement dominates or automation cannot fail safely.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Reliable detection; safe state; human response capacity; understandable handoff; maintenance; workload monitoring.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Higher leverage of human attention, earlier containment and lower repetitive monitoring burden.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Automation bias; deskilling; opaque models; alert overload; unsafe autonomous continuation; humans left with only rare hard cases.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Automation may be justified as labour reduction and can worsen surveillance, pace or job quality; 'human touch' rhetoric is not sufficient.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Modern form resembles human-on-the-loop automation with bounded autonomy, observability and graceful degradation.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Automate only stable, testable routine work; make uncertainty and abnormality explicit; preserve human competence and safe fallback.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Toyota-style machines, andon lights or robots are not required; bounded human-machine allocation is the property.

EL-016 — Process capability, variation measurement and statistical quality control
PROPERTY ID
EL-016
PROPERTY NAME
Process capability, variation measurement and statistical quality control
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Shared Shewhart/Deming statistical-quality ancestry; Toyota SQC from 1949 and TQC from 1961.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
SHARED_QUALITY_ANCESTRY
ORIGINAL FORM
Use control charts and statistical methods to distinguish process behaviour, pursue causes and prevent recurrence.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Anecdotal reactions to variation confuse noise with assignable causes and fail to establish capability.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Tampering, recurrent defects, false improvement and unstable output.
MECHANISM
Define measures and sampling; examine stability and capability; distinguish common/special causes; test countermeasures over time.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Repeated measurable output where variation matters and observations are sufficiently comparable.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For rare or unique work, use structured case evidence and direct tests rather than force a control chart.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Measurement-system validity; enough observations; statistical competence; stable definitions; authority to change systemic causes.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Earlier instability detection, better causal discrimination and qualified process improvement.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Chart ritual; bad data; arbitrary limits; metric gaming; treating statistical control as fitness for use; ignoring rare catastrophic modes.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Lean accounts sometimes appropriate quality ancestry; Deming did not originate JIT or jidoka, and exact influence on A3/5 Whys is unproven.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Expanded from shop-floor control charts to reliability, experimentation and longitudinal outcome evidence.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Use fit-for-purpose variation analysis and capability evidence, explicitly separating stability, customer need and risk.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
A control-chart poster, Deming Prize programme or Six Sigma belt is not required.

EL-017 — Explicit current baseline with controlled revision
PROPERTY ID
EL-017
PROPERTY NAME
Explicit current baseline with controlled revision
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota standardized work (takt, sequence, standard WIP); TWI Job Instruction; later learning-system interpretation.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Specify the current work sequence, pace and standard inventory so it can be taught and managed.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Implicit practice makes deviation, training and improvement impossible to evaluate.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Uncontrolled variation, knowledge loss, untestable change and inconsistent training.
MECHANISM
Version the best-known current method, scope, expected result and exceptions; train it; expose deviations; test and authorise revisions.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Work recurs, consistency matters, multiple people perform it or failure consequences require a known baseline.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For one-off low-risk work, retain a short decision record or checklist rather than a full standard-work sheet.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Worker input; valid current evidence; change and exception authority; review owner/date; ergonomic and safety assessment.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Repeatability, faster training, visible deviation and a platform for improvement.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Immutable procedure; stale paperwork; coercive pace; deskilling; suppressed expert judgement; standards written by distant staff; unauthorised workarounds.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Labour studies show formalisation can reduce autonomy and wellbeing; Toyota doctrine does not prove democratic control.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Reinterpreted from prescribed sequence toward an explicit, testable, provisional baseline with lifecycle controls.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
A scoped, versioned, teachable baseline with exceptions, evidence, local challenge rights, qualification, expiry and retirement.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
A standard-work sheet is optional. Executable checks, runbooks, contracts or annotated examples can serve.

EL-018 — Direct observation combined with traceable system evidence
PROPERTY ID
EL-018
PROPERTY NAME
Direct observation combined with traceable system evidence
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota genchi genbutsu; quality-control measurement; modern observability convergence.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Go to the actual place, see the actual work and grasp facts before deciding.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Reports and abstractions can hide real conditions, while local impressions can also misrepresent distributed systems.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Decision by hearsay, dashboard-only management, executive theatre and local anecdotal causation.
MECHANISM
Observe work and users directly, then triangulate with traces, records, samples and affected-worker testimony; preserve provenance.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Material uncertainty exists about what actually happens or reports conflict with outcomes.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For well-instrumented, low-risk routine work, sample direct observation periodically and investigate exceptions.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Access; non-punitive observation; representative sampling; traceability; skill in distinguishing observation from inference.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Better problem framing, discovery of unrecorded work and stronger causal hypotheses.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Gemba walks as theatre; surveillance; observer effects; anecdotal overreach; privileging visible local work over invisible dependencies.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Direct seeing is neither sufficient nor neutral; distributed software, supply networks and latent harms require instrumentation and testimony.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Mature form treats direct observation as one evidence channel in a provenance-bearing triangulation.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Triangulate actual-work observation, system traces, outcome data and stakeholder accounts; label inference and uncertainty.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
A scheduled gemba walk is not required; contextual inquiry, trace review or incident reconstruction may be better.

EL-019 — Evidence-bound problem solving and countermeasure qualification
PROPERTY ID
EL-019
PROPERTY NAME
Evidence-bound problem solving and countermeasure qualification
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota kaizen/problem solving, SQC/TQC, Ohno's Five Whys; Shewhart/Deming PDSA ancestry.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
HYBRID
ORIGINAL FORM
Investigate an abnormality, ask why, try a countermeasure and standardise successful change.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Symptoms recur because fixes are selected without causal evidence or tested only once.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Template-driven action, single-root stories, recurrence and false learning.
MECHANISM
Define problem and baseline; gather evidence; develop competing causal hypotheses; predict effects; test safely; compare results; check side effects and recurrence.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
The failure is material, recurrent, causally uncertain or costly enough to justify investigation.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For a trivial one-off reversible issue, fix and record the observation; escalate only on recurrence.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Time and skill; data/provenance; ability to test; independent challenge; authority over causes; follow-up owner.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Fewer recurrences, stronger learning and countermeasures that survive outside the initial event.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
A3/5-Whys ritual; confirmation bias; singular root cause; weak controls; no prediction; post hoc success; political exclusion of structural causes.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Format-specific causal evidence is weak; reviews show PDSA often used without core features and RCA often produces weak actions.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Moved from named templates toward explicit scientific reasoning, systems causation and countermeasure qualification.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
A proportional causal-learning loop with competing hypotheses, predictions, safe tests, outcome/side-effect evidence and recurrence review.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
A3 paper, exactly five whys, fishbone or PDCA labels are optional prompts and never evidence.

EL-020 — Improvement sustainment, expiry, retirement and economics
PROPERTY ID
EL-020
PROPERTY NAME
Improvement sustainment, expiry, retirement and economics
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Kaizen follow-up, standardisation after improvement; later sustainment and deimplementation research.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
LEAN_PRODUCTION_GENERALISATION
ORIGINAL FORM
Standardise a successful improvement so gains persist.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Changes decay, create maintenance burden, outlive their premise or continue after value disappears.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Improvement theatre, regression, zombie controls and unbounded process complexity.
MECHANISM
Assign owner, expected benefit, maintenance cost, verification interval, expiry/renewal rule, rollback and retirement evidence.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
A countermeasure changes recurring work or introduces continuing control/maintenance cost.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For ephemeral low-risk changes, define a short observation period and automatic expiry.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Baseline and outcome data; ownership; ability to remove controls; preserved rationale; leadership continuity.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Durable gains, lower bureaucracy, timely removal of obsolete controls and transparent improvement economics.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Audit-only sustainment; permanent controls after risk changes; sunk-cost defence; leadership turnover; benefits not remeasured.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Kaizen-event studies show human outcomes and gains require follow-up; local improvements often lack customer or system outcome evidence.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Added lifecycle, deimplementation and maintenance economics to 'standardise and spread'.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Every material improvement is a governed hypothesis with benefit, burden, review, expiry and retirement path.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Follow-up audits or control plans are implementations; evidence-bearing lifecycle ownership is the property.

EL-021 — Worker capability, training and cross-skilling
PROPERTY ID
EL-021
PROPERTY NAME
Worker capability, training and cross-skilling
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota supervisor/operator development, TWI Job Instruction, standard work and quality-circle traditions.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Train workers and supervisors to perform, recognise abnormality and improve the work.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
A tightly coupled system fails when knowledge is tacit, narrow or unavailable at the point of need.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Skill bottlenecks, unsafe improvisation, slow recovery and dependence on a few experts.
MECHANISM
Define required capabilities; train through demonstration and practice; verify competence; cross-skill for response; preserve coaching time.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Work recurs, failures require local response, or capacity flexibility depends on transferable skill.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For rare low-risk work, retain accessible guidance and an expert escalation path rather than broad certification.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Paid training time; qualified instructors; humane workload; current baselines; opportunities to practise; recognition of expertise.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Safer execution, flexible capacity, faster abnormality response and more credible local improvement.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Training as compliance; skill matrices without ability; unpaid improvement burden; multiskilling used only to cut staffing; obsolete instruction.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Participation and cross-skilling can intensify work and widen responsibility without control, pay or security.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
From job instruction and narrow shop skills toward socio-technical capability, coaching, recovery practice and knowledge continuity.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Maintain verified capability and cross-coverage for material work, paired with authority, workload protection and fair recognition.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
TWI cards, dojos and skill matrices are optional; demonstrated capability and coverage are the property.

EL-022 — Safe voice, participation and bounded change authority
PROPERTY ID
EL-022
PROPERTY NAME
Safe voice, participation and bounded change authority
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota respect-for-human doctrine, suggestion systems, quality circles, andon and NUMMI involvement.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Workers participate in operating and improving the workshop and can call attention to abnormalities.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
People closest to work possess information but cannot safely expose or change problems.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Suppressed alarms, learned helplessness, management blindness and pseudo-participation.
MECHANISM
Provide non-punitive signal, response and appeal channels; specify local/team/cross-functional change rights; protect dissent and stop authority.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Front-line observation is necessary for quality, safety or improvement and management cannot centrally see conditions.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For simple low-risk work, a direct owner contact and documented response is enough.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Employment/psychological safety; response obligation; workload and time; representation; transparent decisions; escalation above local management.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Earlier problem exposure, richer evidence, faster local correction and greater legitimacy.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Suggestion quotas; ignored andons; consultation without decision rights; retaliation; responsibility without resources; surveillance.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Toyota and Lean rhetoric often labels involvement 'empowerment' while pace, staffing and strategic decisions remain managerial.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Mature form distinguishes voice, information, consent, local change authority, stop authority and bargaining power.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
A governed rights-and-response system: safe signal, bounded local action, appeal, documented disposition and protection against workload transfer.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Suggestion boxes, quality circles and daily huddles are optional; real voice and decision rights are the property.

EL-023 — Employment security, trust and fair distribution of productivity gains
PROPERTY ID
EL-023
PROPERTY NAME
Employment security, trust and fair distribution of productivity gains
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota's post-1950 labour compact and later joint declarations; NUMMI employment-security practice.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
SOCIO_TECHNICAL_CONTEXT_PROPERTY
ORIGINAL FORM
Link productivity improvement with stable employment, working conditions and labour-management trust.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Workers rationally conceal improvement opportunities when improvement threatens jobs, workload or bargaining position.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Trust destruction, sabotage, hidden problems, fear-based compliance and 'lean and mean' layoffs.
MECHANISM
Set credible no-layoff or redeployment rules for improvement gains where feasible; negotiate workload; share gains; protect reporting and transition.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Improvement solicits worker knowledge, reduces labour content or changes job design materially.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For small changes with no employment/workload effect, state that boundary and monitor cumulative effects.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Credible management commitment; financial capacity; worker representation/voice; redeployment and training; transparent gain accounting.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
More truthful problem exposure, cooperation, capability retention and lower fear.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Promises withdrawn in downturns; gains captured by owners; redeployment becomes intensification; insiders protected while suppliers/contractors absorb risk.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Toyota's 1950 dispute and wider Lean layoffs show security is not inherent; national institutions and firm economics matter.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Moved from implicit Japanese/NUMMI compact to explicit socio-technical prerequisite and fair-transition requirement.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Before labour-saving improvement, define employment, workload, compensation, redeployment and supplier effects; treat trust as evidence-bearing governance.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Respect-for-people slogans, town halls and culture statements do not establish security or fair gains.

EL-024 — Management responsibility for system conditions and escalation response
PROPERTY ID
EL-024
PROPERTY NAME
Management responsibility for system conditions and escalation response
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota supervisory/management system, TQC and later TPS learning-system interpretations.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Managers create conditions, respond to abnormalities and support workers in solving problems.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Local teams are held accountable for causes created by staffing, incentives, architecture, contracts or policy.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Blame shifting, repeated local fixes, ignored escalation and transformation decay.
MECHANISM
Assign accountable system owners; require timely response; remove cross-boundary impediments; fund capability/buffers; review whether incentives create the failure.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
A problem exceeds local authority, recurs across teams or reflects structural operating conditions.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For a bounded local issue, delegate action with a clear escalation threshold.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Authority over resources and policy; transparent response expectations; independent review; outcome and labour measures.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Structural causes addressed, credible stop/voice systems and sustained improvement.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Leader gemba theatre; delegation without resources; hero intervention; changing priorities; local blame; executive metrics conflict with doctrine.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Proponent accounts often make management commitment an unfalsifiable explanation for success or failure.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Shifted from supervisor response to explicit system ownership, governance and accountability for incentives and preconditions.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Leaders own the conditions they control, respond to escalations, arbitrate cross-system trade-offs and are measured on outcomes, resilience and worker effects.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Leader standard work, gemba walks and coaching kata are optional; accountable system condition ownership is the property.

EL-025 — Whole-system optimisation and proxy/metric governance
PROPERTY ID
EL-025
PROPERTY NAME
Whole-system optimisation and proxy/metric governance
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
TPS anti-overproduction and system flow; Lean 'optimise the whole'; modern operations and software measurement.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
HYBRID
ORIGINAL FORM
Subordinate local production to the needs of the downstream process and whole value stream.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Teams game local throughput, cycle time, defect, utilisation or productivity metrics while system outcomes deteriorate.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Local optimisation, Goodhart effects, hidden queues, quality sacrifice and worker harm.
MECHANISM
Use a balanced causal measurement set; define system boundary and countermetrics; avoid individual competition; audit gaming and revise definitions.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Metrics affect incentives or decisions across interacting components.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For a small team with direct outcome visibility, use a few narrative/quantitative checks rather than a dashboard programme.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Outcome model; stable metric definitions; cross-boundary data; qualitative challenge; no punishment for adverse signals.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Better decisions, fewer proxy pathologies and alignment of flow, quality, resilience, value and wellbeing.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Dashboard proliferation; composite-score opacity; denominator manipulation; item splitting; suppressed incidents; cross-context ranking; measurement burden.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Lean measures are frequently used for labour control; DORA and SPACE explicitly warn against single metrics and inappropriate comparisons.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
From local flow and waste measures to multidimensional system indicators, countermetrics and explicit anti-gaming governance.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Treat metrics as revisable evidence, not targets or truth; pair throughput with quality, outcome, resilience and human effects.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Obeya boards, SQDC panels, burndowns or DORA dashboards are optional; decision-valid measurement is the property.

EL-026 — Equipment, service and delivery-system reliability and maintainability
PROPERTY ID
EL-026
PROPERTY NAME
Equipment, service and delivery-system reliability and maintainability
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
TPS equipment availability and preventive maintenance; TPM quality ancestry; modern reliability engineering.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
SHARED_QUALITY_ANCESTRY
ORIGINAL FORM
Maintain equipment capability so low-WIP flow is not repeatedly interrupted.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Tight flow exposes breakdowns, but exposure without reliability capability creates service failure.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Unplanned downtime, repeated emergency repair, unsafe degradation and buffer depletion.
MECHANISM
Identify critical assets/services; monitor failure and degradation; perform risk-based preventive/predictive maintenance; design for diagnosis, repair and recovery.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Failure interrupts flow materially, has safety/quality consequences or consumes strategic buffers.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For noncritical, cheap and rapidly replaceable components, use run-to-failure with spares and a tested replacement path.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Criticality model; maintenance windows and capacity; skilled responders; spares; configuration history; feedback from incidents.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Higher availability, lower variability, safer operation and smaller required buffers.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Maintenance theatre; over-maintenance; bad predictive models; deferred maintenance to hit throughput; hidden technical debt; single-expert dependence.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Low inventory can convert ordinary failures into crises, while TPM programmes can become checklist bundles without reliability economics.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
From factory preventive maintenance to service reliability, observability, error budgets, graceful degradation and recovery engineering.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Choose maintenance and recovery policy by criticality, failure distribution, consequence and replacement economics.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
TPM pillars, maintenance boards and autonomous-maintenance checklists are optional implementations.

EL-027 — Supplier and dependency capability development with fair risk allocation
PROPERTY ID
EL-027
PROPERTY NAME
Supplier and dependency capability development with fair risk allocation
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota supplier associations, quality/TPS training and interfirm learning network.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Develop suppliers' quality, delivery and improvement capability rather than inspect or bargain only on price.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
End-to-end performance is limited by external capability and incentives outside the focal organisation.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Recurring supplier defects, unstable replenishment, adversarial cost transfer and weak joint learning.
MECHANISM
Share requirements and knowledge; jointly solve capability gaps; use fair contracts, gain/risk sharing and escalation; measure supplier health as well as price.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
A dependency is recurrent, strategically material and improvable through relationship-specific learning.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For commodity, low-risk purchases, use clear specifications, diversified sources and routine qualification rather than a development programme.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Long-term relationship; technical openness; bargaining fairness; supplier capacity; protection of proprietary knowledge; exit alternatives.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Higher quality, faster learning, less disruption and lower total cost.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Coercive price reduction; unpaid improvement; lock-in; focal-firm appropriation of gains; supplier overinvestment; lower-tier labour harm.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Toyota partnership narratives can obscure power asymmetry; synchronisation may transfer buffers, capital and risk to suppliers.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Mature form adds supplier viability, fair gain/risk allocation, labour standards, lower-tier visibility and recovery planning.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Develop critical dependency capability only where joint value exceeds coordination and lock-in cost, with explicit fairness and resilience safeguards.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Supplier kaizen workshops, scorecards or associations are optional; joint capability and fair allocation are the property.

EL-028 — Multi-tier dependency visibility and resilience segmentation
PROPERTY ID
EL-028
PROPERTY NAME
Multi-tier dependency visibility and resilience segmentation
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Post-2011 Toyota supply-chain mapping and broader resilience research.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
REACTION_TO_LEAN
ORIGINAL FORM
After disruption, identify lower-tier sites, affected items, inventories and restoration alternatives.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Tier-one visibility hides common sub-tier dependencies, concentration and long recovery paths.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Correlated disruption, unknown single points of failure, slow recovery and false diversification.
MECHANISM
Map critical item/site dependencies beyond the first tier; classify by lead time, substitutability and consequence; prepare restoration, alternate design/source and data-maintenance routines.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
A dependency can stop a material outcome, has long recovery, opaque tiers or geographic/technical concentration.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For low-criticality, rapidly substitutable inputs, record vendor/category and test replacement rather than map the whole network.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Supplier cooperation; data governance; item-site identity; criticality model; alternative qualification; regular updates.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Earlier disruption recognition, targeted buffers and faster alternate sourcing or redesign.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Huge stale maps; supplier confidentiality conflict; false sense of control; concentration persists despite visibility; mapping cost exceeds value.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Mapping does not itself create resilience and can shift reporting burden to small suppliers.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
From JIT synchronisation to segmented JIT/JIC, virtual dualisation, critical-stock and recoverability design.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Maintain decision-useful deep-tier visibility only for critical dependencies, coupled to buffer, alternate and recovery action.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
A proprietary supply-chain map or Toyota RESCUE system is not required; a critical-dependency register may suffice.

  1. S037
  2. S040

Methods for validating supplier-reported lower-tier data and common-cause dependence remain immature.
IMPROVEMENT AUTHORITY
SYSTEM_OWNER
FLOW RESILIENCE PROFILE

  1. EL-027

  1. Does the system know which lower-tier dependencies can stop the outcome and how long recovery takes?
  2. Is visibility linked to an alternate, buffer, redesign or restoration decision?
  3. Can low-criticality dependencies follow a cheap path instead of exhaustive mapping?

EL-029 — Set-based option preservation and delayed irreversible convergence
PROPERTY ID
EL-029
PROPERTY NAME
Set-based option preservation and delayed irreversible convergence
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toyota product-development research and set-based concurrent engineering.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
LEAN_PRODUCT_DEVELOPMENT_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Explore ranges of alternatives, communicate constraints and narrow sets as feasibility evidence accumulates.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Early point decisions create lock-in, late redesign and fragile optimisation under uncertainty.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Premature convergence, expensive reversal and loss of feasible alternatives.
MECHANISM
Map design space and constraints; keep multiple feasible options; run discriminating tests; eliminate options with evidence; commit at the last responsible point.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Uncertainty, coupling and irreversibility are high enough that a wrong early choice is costly and parallel learning is affordable.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
When the decision is reversible, familiar or low-coupling, choose one option and preserve a fallback note.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Capacity for parallel analysis; explicit decision criteria; integration/trade-off knowledge; convergence deadlines; ownership.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Reduced late rework, better trade-offs and more robust commitment.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Unbounded option proliferation; delayed decisions; duplicated work; superficial sets; hidden resource cost; no convergence rule.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Industrial implementation maturity and causal performance evidence remain limited; not every domain benefits.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Separated from manufacturing waste elimination and reframed as an uncertainty/option-value mechanism.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Preserve only decision-relevant alternatives until evidence changes expected regret; record eliminated options and convergence rationale.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Trade-off curves, set matrices and obeya rooms are optional; evidence-calibrated option preservation is the property.
Status at this source freeze
CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
EVIDENCE STRENGTH
MODERATE_CASE_BASED
PRIMARY SOURCES
  1. S049
  2. S050
CRITICAL SOURCES
  1. S051
  2. S052
EMPIRICAL OR DOMAIN EVIDENCE
  1. S049
  2. S052
OPEN QUESTIONS
Strong comparative causal evidence outside automotive/product design remains limited.
IMPROVEMENT AUTHORITY
CROSS_FUNCTIONAL
FLOW RESILIENCE PROFILE
Exploration intentionally creates parallel WIP; favour it where option value exceeds queue and coordination cost. Conflicts with premature flow optimisation.
POSSIBLE CONFLICTING PROPERTY
  1. EL-004
  2. EL-005
  3. EL-007
  4. EL-036
QUESTIONS FOR REPOSITORY AUDIT
  1. Is uncertainty and irreversibility high enough to justify preserving alternatives?
  2. What evidence eliminates an option, and when must convergence occur?
  3. Would parallel exploration reduce expected rework or merely duplicate effort?

EL-030 — Cross-functional product-development integration and reusable trade-off knowledge
PROPERTY ID
EL-030
PROPERTY NAME
Cross-functional product-development integration and reusable trade-off knowledge
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Comparative automotive product-development research and Toyota LPD interpretations.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
LEAN_PRODUCT_DEVELOPMENT_LINEAGE
ORIGINAL FORM
Integrate product, process and organisational knowledge under strong project leadership; reuse constraints and trade-offs.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Functional silos discover incompatibility late and repeatedly relearn design constraints.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Late engineering changes, handoff loss, manufacturing/service incompatibility and repeated analysis.
MECHANISM
Create cross-functional decision forums; maintain constraint interfaces, trade-off evidence and reusable knowledge; give project leadership authority to resolve conflicts.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Product outcomes couple multiple disciplines, lifecycle stages or downstream operations.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For a small single-discipline change, direct peer review and a concise decision record suffice.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Representative disciplines; decision authority; shared models; knowledge stewardship; incentives for lifecycle rather than functional optimisation.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Faster convergence, fewer late changes and compounding design knowledge.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Meeting overload; heavyweight bureaucracy; knowledge repositories without use; dominant function suppresses dissent; outdated trade-off curves.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Automotive evidence may not transfer; LPD lacks agreed definition and complete coherent implementation evidence.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
From heavyweight project management toward digital thread, model-based trade-offs, concurrent assurance and learning reuse.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Integrate affected disciplines at consequential decisions and preserve reusable evidence at interfaces—not every participant in every meeting.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Obeya rooms, chief-engineer titles and trade-off curves are optional implementations.

EL-031 — Software delivery flow with automation, observability, reliability and recovery
PROPERTY ID
EL-031
PROPERTY NAME
Software delivery flow with automation, observability, reliability and recovery
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Lean software/Kanban translations combined with Continuous Delivery, DevOps and reliability engineering.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
HYBRID
ORIGINAL FORM
Translate flow, small batches, quality-at-source and WIP limitation into software work.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Software changes wait in queues, integrate late, fail opaquely or reach production without safe recovery.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Large risky releases, slow feedback, hidden operational defects and speed-at-the-expense-of-stability.
MECHANISM
Limit active work; integrate frequently; automate build/test/deploy; observe production; use progressive delivery, rollback and incident learning; validate user outcomes.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Software changes are frequent enough that manual release and delayed integration impose risk or delay.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For infrequent low-risk software, use reproducible builds, basic automated tests and a documented release/recovery path.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Version control; trustworthy tests; environment/configuration control; observability; on-call/recovery capability; product outcome feedback.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Faster and safer change, shorter learning cycles and reduced release blast radius.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Pipeline theatre; flaky tests; metric gaming; developer burnout; rapid delivery of low-value work; fragile platforms; operations burden hidden.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Software evidence is largely observational and case-based; factory metaphors miss discovery, architecture and invisible cognitive work.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Kanban's flow ideas were hybridised with software-native automation, reliability, security and observability.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Optimise sustainable software change across throughput and instability, with small reversible changes, outcome validation and humane operations.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
A kanban board, daily deployment or DORA dashboard is not required; reliable, observable and reversible delivery capability is the property.

EL-032 — Portfolio selection, stopping and retirement of low-value work
PROPERTY ID
EL-032
PROPERTY NAME
Portfolio selection, stopping and retirement of low-value work
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Lean value/waste generalisation, product-development learning and modern product/flow management.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING
ORIGINAL FORM
Eliminate work that does not create value and stop overproduction.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Organisations optimise execution while accepting too much low-value work and retaining obsolete products, controls or commitments.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
Portfolio congestion, zombie work, sunk-cost continuation and capacity starvation.
MECHANISM
Set admission, continuation and retirement criteria; compare expected value and learning against total cost; stop, defer or archive work explicitly.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
Demand exceeds capacity, work ages without evidence, or a value hypothesis fails.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
For a tiny backlog, review informally at a defined interval and close stale items.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Outcome evidence; total-cost visibility; authority to stop; reversible archival/transition; stakeholder communication.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Freed capacity, lower WIP, less maintenance burden and focus on consequential outcomes.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Politically convenient cancellation; short-termism; abandoned users; hidden transition costs; gaming by redefining work as mandatory.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Waste language can erase care, resilience or exploratory work whose value is indirect or delayed.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
From eliminating production waste to governing portfolio entry, continuation, deimplementation and retirement.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Stop or retire work only through an evidence-bearing decision that accounts for beneficiary, option value, transition and external costs.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Backlog purges, stop-start-continue workshops and portfolio kanban are optional.

EL-033 — Universal waste elimination as the master objective
PROPERTY ID
EL-033
PROPERTY NAME
Universal waste elimination as the master objective
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Later Lean simplification of Ohno's waste categories and Lean Thinking.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
TOOLKIT_CARICATURE
ORIGINAL FORM
Identify and eliminate all non-value-adding activity.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Real waste can consume capacity, obscure defects and delay value.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
None as a universal master objective; the maxim itself can create harm.
MECHANISM
As historically deployed, classify activity as waste and remove it.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
NO GENERAL TRIGGER. Specific waste removal must be justified through EL-001, EL-002, EL-008 and outcome evidence.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
Name the concrete failure mode and remove only the activity that causes it; do not launch generic waste hunts.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Valid value model, full system boundary, labour/resilience analysis and evidence that the activity is avoidable.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Can remove genuine delay, rework or unnecessary effort when narrowly applied.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Removes resilience, learning, care, maintenance, worker discretion or necessary redundancy; becomes headcount reduction.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Waste is easier to name than value; universal elimination ignores variability, option value and externalities.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Superseded by explicit outcome, queue, buffer, reliability and portfolio mechanisms.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
No independent property. Ask which failure mode the alleged waste causes and what protective function it may serve.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Waste walks, seven/eight-waste posters and red-tag events are not evidence.

EL-034 — Minimum or zero inventory everywhere
PROPERTY ID
EL-034
PROPERTY NAME
Minimum or zero inventory everywhere
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Toolkit caricature of JIT and low-stock Lean programmes.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
TOOLKIT_CARICATURE
ORIGINAL FORM
Drive inventory toward zero to expose problems and release capital.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Excess stock can hide instability, delay feedback and consume capital.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
None at universal scope; the maxim creates unbuffered risk.
MECHANISM
Remove inventory and rely on replenishment or expediting.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
NO GENERAL TRIGGER. Inventory reduction is justified only after component-specific buffer economics.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
Measure criticality, lead time, obsolescence and recovery; remove only demonstrably excess stock.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Reliable replenishment; alternate capacity; bounded demand/lead-time variability; recovery capability.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Lower carrying cost and faster defect feedback within a safe operating region.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Stockouts, production/service interruption, supplier pressure, emergency freight and correlated disruption.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Toyota sources specify minimum necessary stock, not zero; empirical performance is often concave and disruption research rejects binary JIT/JIC.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Replaced by EL-008 and EL-028: segmented buffer portfolios and dependency resilience.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
NO GENERAL PROPERTY; choose inventory by total expected cost and resilience.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Kanban quantity cuts and inventory-reduction targets are not inherently beneficial.

  3. Could the same benefit come from reliability or setup improvement without reducing protection?

EL-035 — Maximum utilisation and zero slack
PROPERTY ID
EL-035
PROPERTY NAME
Maximum utilisation and zero slack
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Mass-production efficiency logic and cost-cutting Lean appropriations, not a defensible TPS invariant.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
TOOLKIT_CARICATURE
ORIGINAL FORM
Keep labour and equipment continuously busy and remove idle capacity.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Visible idle resources can be costly, but under variability utilisation is not throughput.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
None at universal scope.
MECHANISM
Increase load, cut staffing and fill all available capacity.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
NO GENERAL TRIGGER. High planned utilisation may be acceptable only with low variability and cheap delay.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
Measure queues and service risk; release spare capacity only where it has no response or resilience function.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Near-deterministic arrivals/service, low failure consequence and external buffers.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
May lower unit cost in a narrow stable operating region.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Nonlinear queues, burnout, no improvement/response time, brittle recovery and hidden overtime.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Operations science shows capacity is one of the required variability buffers; labour evidence links intensification to harm.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Rejected and replaced by queue-aware capacity plus explicit strategic slack.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
Target sustainable service and total cost, not maximum busy time.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Utilisation dashboards, staffing-to-takt formulas and efficiency targets do not establish value.

EL-039 — Universal supplier synchronisation, concentration or single sourcing
PROPERTY ID
EL-039
PROPERTY NAME
Universal supplier synchronisation, concentration or single sourcing
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Overgeneralisation of Toyota supplier/JIT coordination.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
TOOLKIT_CARICATURE
ORIGINAL FORM
Tightly synchronise suppliers and reduce duplicated stocks/sources.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Uncoordinated supply can create inventory and quality problems.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
None at universal scope; synchronisation can magnify common-cause failure.
MECHANISM
Concentrate volume, shorten schedules and reduce supplier/stock redundancy.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
NO GENERAL TRIGGER. Deep synchronisation is justified only where capability gains exceed concentration and disruption risk.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
Use clear interfaces, qualified alternates and ordinary supplier management for nonstrategic inputs.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
Supplier viability; visibility; fair contracts; recovery; substitutability; geographic/technical risk analysis.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
May improve quality learning and transaction efficiency in stable, high-trust relationships.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Lock-in, supplier fragility, lower-tier single points, bargaining abuse and correlated disruption.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Post-2011 evidence and JIT risk studies show synchronisation/centralisation can raise disruption exposure.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED

EL-040 — Immutable standardisation and procedure compliance
PROPERTY ID
EL-040
PROPERTY NAME
Immutable standardisation and procedure compliance
HISTORICAL ORIGIN
Taylorist/managerial appropriation of standard work and audit-based Lean deployments.
LINEAGE CLASSIFICATION
MANAGEMENT_ADOPTION
ORIGINAL FORM
Define one best way and require conformance.
PROBLEM IT ADDRESSED
Variation can be harmful, but frozen procedure prevents adaptation and learning.
FAILURE MODE IT TRIES TO PREVENT
None as an immutable rule.
MECHANISM
Central authors prescribe and audit compliance; deviations are treated as worker failure.
TRIGGER OR CONTEXT
NO GENERAL TRIGGER. Safety-critical immutable constraints may exist, but even they require controlled review.
NON TRIGGER OR CHEAP PATH
Use EL-017: a current baseline with explicit exceptions, change rights and review.
DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS
If strict compliance is temporarily necessary: validated hazard case, scope, independent review, override/escalation and expiry.
EXPECTED ENGINEERING PAYOFF
Can reduce unsafe variation for stable, validated critical steps.
KNOWN FAILURE MODES
Stale procedure, hidden workarounds, deskilling, loss of expertise, coercion and inability to respond to novel conditions.
IMPORTANT CRITICISMS
Worker studies link workflow formalisation and autonomy loss to poorer outcomes; mature Lean interpretations describe standards as improvable baselines.
HOW THE PROPERTY EVOLVED
Rejected and replaced by lifecycle-controlled baseline plus human judgement and change authority.
MATURE OR EVOLVED FORM
No immutable general standard; use risk-calibrated constraints within a versioned and reviewable baseline.
CEREMONY VS PROPERTY
Standard-work sheets, SOP audits and conformance scores are not proof of capability.
